[\[1b.1\]{D1346}]@##/_/rgya gar skad du/_pa ra ma A di bud+d+ha ud+d+h+r-i ta shrI kA la tsakra nA ma tan+t+ra rA dzA/_bod skad du/_mchog gi dang po'i sangs rgyas las phyung ba rgyud kyi rgyal po dpal dus kyi 'khor 
[\[1b.2\]]lo zhes bya ba/_dpal dus kyi 'khor lo la phyag 'tshal lo/_/thams cad mkhyen pa ye shes sku dang nyin mor byed pa'i sku ste pad+ma'i 'dab ma rgyas pa'i spyan/_/sangs rgyas seng ge'i khri la 
[\[1b.3\]]bzhugs pa lha mchog rnams kyis btud la rgyal po zla ba bzang po yis/_/mgo bos rab tu phyag 'tshal lag pa'i pad+ma sbyar ba yan lag mchog la bzhag nas zhus pa ni/_/rnal 'byor dpal 
[\[1b.4\]]ldan dus 'khor ka phreng ldan pa'i a 'dus la ste mi rnams dgrol ba'i don du'o/_1_/stong pa ye shes kyang ste thig le mchog mchog rdo rje 'dzin pa sangs rgyas lha dang lha min yang /_/phyi dang 
[\[1b.5\]]lus dang gzhan la yang ste rang bzhin rnams la skyes bu nyi shu lnga pa'i bdag nyid dang /_/lus la sna tshogs tshad dang srid pa gsum gyi bkod pa lha dang lha min rnams kyi longs spyod dang /_/
[\[2a\]]
[\[2a.1\]]dkyil 'khor dang ni dbang ste skabs gsum pa dang mi yi bla mas 'di dag yang dag bshad du gsol/_2_/zla ba bzang po khyod la bdag mgu gang zhig khyod kyis dris pa 'di ni rab mchog lha rnams 
[\[2a.2\]]dang /_/mi dang srin po lha min klu dang chags bral thub mchog rigs rnams dag gis shes pa min pas so/_/mya ngan 'das pa la sogs 'dzin ma'i mthar thug tshig gi bgrod pa dang bcas sbyor ba mtha' dag 
[\[2a.3\]]ni/_/lus dbus su ste dkyil 'khor dag dang dbang ni bshad par bya yis mi yi bdag po bzang po nyon/_3_/dus kyis stong pa rnams las rlung dang 'bar byed chu dang 'dzin ma gling dang ri bo rgya mtsho dang /_/rgyu skar 
[\[2a.4\]]dang ni nyi zla skar ma gza' yi tshogs dang drang srong lha dang 'byung po klu rnams dang /_/dud 'gro skye gnas rnam pa bzhi dang sna tshogs sa dang 'og tu mi rnams dang ni dmyal ba yang /_/stong pa'i dbus su yang dag 'byung ste chu 
[\[2a.5\]]la lan tshwa dang ni sgo nga'i dbus su sgong skyes bzhin/_4_/lus dang ye shes nam mkha' dang ni rlung dang me dang chu dang sa dang brtan dang g.yo ba dang /_/lha ni mthong ba skyed min dag dang srid pa rnam bcur gsungs pa rdo rje yi ni 
[\[2b\]]
[\[2b.1\]]lus dag las/_/sngags kyi skye gnas dag ni 'byung bar 'gyur zhing mi yi bdag po slar yang 'di nyid du grol te/_/de ltar gang gis yang dag rig pa de ni phyugs min sems kyis kun du rtog pa dag las grol/_5_/g.yon gyi yan lag tu ni dkar po 'bar ba 'gro ba'i 
[\[2b.2\]]bdud rtsi'i cha dang g.yas su dmar po'i mdog_/sgra gcan dus me zla ba nyi ma ri bong can gyi bu dang sa skyes dkar po bla ma dang /_/mjug [{]rings [,]ring [}]dman pa char dang glog dang chu dang rtse mo can te 'bar ba rnams ni zung bdun po/_/'jig rten la ni gnas pa nyid de mun pa 
[\[2b.3\]]med pa zung gcig dag ni mun pa'i mtha' la'o/_6_/rgyab tu ser po yang ni skar ma lha gzhu sa ste de ni rnam bzhi gnyis kyi dbye bas so/_/srog ces bya ba gcig pu'i rlung ni rnam bcur 'gyur te spyi bo rtsa ba dag dang mdun du'o/_/dbus kyi rdo rje'i steng du sa bon rang gi nus pa yon 
[\[2b.4\]]tan tshogs dang bcas pa 'og tu gnas/_/kun du ye shes dang ni stong pa'o zhi ba gnas dang bcas pa dngos po kun gyis rnam par grol/_7_/a sogs sum cu'i dbyangs gang ha ya ra wa lar ldan de dag zla ba'i cha dang nyin byed kyang /_/ka la sogs pa'i sde rnams dbyangs dang bcas 
[\[2b.5\]]pa nyin zhag stong drug me yi tshad kyis spyod par byed/_/ha sogs dbyangs rnams dag kyang chu tshod lha min gdengs can 'byung po'i skye gnas dag kyang sngags shes pa/_/a sogs ka sogs ldan pa dag las de ltar khams gsum dag ni nges par skyed 'gyur mi yi bdag_8_/dbyangs 
[\[2b.6\]]ni ka tsa Ta pa tar ldan \u0f88\u0f90 la sogs pa yang dag sbyar ba rnams kyi skye ba'i gnas/_/mgrin rkan steng gi cha ni mkha' rlung bsreg bya las skyes mchu dang so ni chu dang sa dag go/_/a sogs rnams ni gtsug tor 'khor lo snying mgrin mgo bo lte ba'i 'khor lo dag la rim pa ste/_/sna 
[\[2b.7\]]tshogs gnag dmar ri bong can dang gser 'dra phung po khams kyi lhag pa'i lha dang ldan pa la'o/_9_/rlung gi mtha' nas lhun po'i mtshams su dmyal ba gdengs can grong khyer la ni dpag tshad 'bum phrag gnyis/_/lhun po'i tshad ni 'bum phrag gcig ste gza' yi tshogs 
[\[3a\]]
[\[3a.1\]]kyi gnas nas stong phrag dag ni nyi shu lnga/_/mgrin pa'o gdong la lnga bcu brtan pa'i go 'phang g.yo med dag la de bzhin nyid du nyi shu lnga/_/de yi phyi rol stong pa gcig pu srid pa gsum bral yon tan med cing de nyid kyis dman pa'o/_10_/rlung gi mtha' 
[\[3a.2\]]nas rlung gi mtshams dang brtan pa 'dzin ma'i gzhi la gling dang ri dang rgya mtsho rnams/_/bzhi phyed 'bum phrag gnyis te rtse mo can dang g.yo ba'i dkyil 'khor dpag tshad dag ni 'bum phrag gnyis/_/dbus na lhun po gang gi steng du khyim gyi 'khor lo skar bcas nyin dang mtshan 
[\[3a.3\]]mor 'khor ba ste/_/cha drug la ni 'bum phrag gnyis gnyis srid gsum mtha' dag dus kyi sbyor ba dag las rab tu skyes/_11_/'jig rten khams kyi kun nas 'khor yug tu ni thad ka'i tshad kyi sum 'gyur du yang 'gyur ba ste/_/sa yi 'khor yug 'bum phrag gsum dang 
[\[3a.4\]]chu me rlung rnams kyi ni drug dgu nyi ma rim pas so/_/mi yi dbang po phyi rol srid pa gsum gyi gnas la dpag tshad 'bum phrag gcig nyid gang yin pa/_/de ni sa yi steng gi gnas su lus la rang rang dag gi tshad kyis yang dag khru gang ngo /_12_/phra rab brgyad rnams dag gis phra mo 'di 
[\[3a.5\]]gcig phra mo brgyad kyis skra yi rtse mo phra mo ste/_/'di rnams kyis ni ske tshe shig dang nas te ltos 'gro nas kyis sor te sor mo nyi ma ni/_/zung gis khru'o khru bzhis gzhu ste 'dir ni stong phrag gnyis kyi gzhu yis rgyang grags dag tu 'gyur/_/rgyang grags bzhi rnams dag gis dpag 
[\[3a.6\]]tshad de yis lha yul sa dang nam mkha' dag la tshad yin no/_13_/dang por chos bzang bskal pa zung ni zung dang ldan pa dag ste tshangs dang 'jig rten 'das pa dang /_/dpal gyi bskal pa dkar po'i bskal pa yang ste shin tu dbang gyur gnas nas 'dod pa bcu gcig go/_/gzugs 
[\[3a.7\]]med dag ni a sogs bzhi po ha 'khrig bral ba rnams te i la sogs pa bcu drug gzugs/_/ha sogs la mtha' yang ni 'dod pa rnam bcur rab tu gsal te gsal byed gcig pa ka sa'o/_14_/srid pa sum cu rtsa gcig dag gis 'dir ni srid gsum du 'gyur khams kyi dbye 
[\[3b\]]
[\[3b.1\]]bas khams gsum mo/_/srid gsum ma lus 'di yang rdo rje gsum du 'gyur te a sogs ka sogs rab tu dbye bas so/_/rin chen snang nas gseg ma'i chu ni dmyal bar nges par brjod de bye ma'i chu ni gnyis pa'o/_/'dam gyi chu dang mi bzad du ba dag dang me dang mun pa dang ni [(]du [,]ngu [)]
[\[3b.2\]]'bod bdun pa dang /_15_/lan tshwa chang dang chu dang 'o ma zho dang mar dang sbrang rtsi'i rgya mtsho bdun te ri bo ni/_/'od sngon man+da ra ri ni Sha Ta dang nor bu'i 'od dang dro Na bsil ri rdo rje'o/_/gling ni zla ba 'od dkar dag na rab mchog ku sha dang ni mi'am ci dang khrung khrung dang /_/drag po yang ni 
[\[3b.3\]]longs spyod sa ste bdun pa 'dzam bu mi rnams gnas pa dag ni las kyi sa/_16_/shar du rlung ni shin tu zlum por 'gyur te mi yi bdag po lho ru me ni gru gsum mo/_/byang du chu ni phyed kyi zla ba dag dang nub tu gser mchog sa ni chu yi gter gyi zur/_/ri rab lhun po stong pa'i rnam pa rdo rje 
[\[3b.4\]]mchog gi rang bzhin dkyil 'khor rnams kyi dbus su ste/_/sa yi steng du dpag tshad stong phrag rnams ni ri dang klu dang gza' dang phyogs rnams dag tu 'gyur/_17_/lhun po yi ni steng gi rgya dang sa gzhi dag gi gnas la dpag tshad rnams kyi stong phrag ni/_/lnga bcu bcu drug dag go 
[\[3b.5\]]gcig ni rab mchog sa yi gzhi la khor yug dag gi yang dag ste/_/rwa ni lnga po rnams dag steng dang sa gzhi dag gi gnas la phyogs kun du ni khor yug go/_/de yi phyi rol gling dang ri bo dag dang chu gter rnams te phyogs rnams kun du me dang rlung /_18_/chu gter drug po thams cad dag

[\[4a\]]
[\[4a.1\]]lhun po'i phyogs rnams kun du sa yi dkyil 'khor la son gnas rnams kun dang nye ba'i gnas/_/zhing dang 'dun pa dag dang 'dus pa can dang ro khang gnas ni me dang rlung gi mtshams kyi mthar/_20_/shar du brgya byin mer ni me lha gshin rje bden bral nub phyogs 
[\[4a.2\]]su ni gshin rje srin po chu/_/rlung dang nor sbyin 'phrog byed phyogs su rlung dang gnod sbyin drag po steng gi cha dang 'og tu yang /_/tshangs dang khyab 'jug mtshon ldan 'khor gyi skye bo dang bcas rang rang phyogs ni srung zhing skyong ba ste/_/de dag dbus su dus kyi 'khor lo rgyal ba mchog gi 
[\[4a.3\]]skyed mdzad gzhom du med pa rdo rje'i sku/_21_/lhun po'i rgyab kyi phyogs rnams su ni sa gzhi dag la lha min rnams kyis rgyal bar dka' ba 'khor/_/gang du chos ni rnam par nyams shing rtsod dus 'jug pa de dang der ni rab tu bgrod par byed/_/kla klo rnams kyang g.yul du bcom ste rang 
[\[4a.4\]]gi chos la dgod pa byas nas mdun du rnam par rgyu/_/rdzogs ldan gsum ldan gnyis ldan dag dang rtsod dus gzhan ni dus kyi sbyor ba dag gis 'byung bar 'gyur/_22_/dum bu gang du stobs ldan 'khor lo can de rab tu zhugs pa der ni rdzogs ldan dus bgrod de/_/rgyal po'i rgyab tu 
[\[4a.5\]]gsum ldan rtsod pa'i dus kyang mdun dag tu ste gnyis kyi bar du gnyis ldan no/_/dus rnams dag gi lo yi tshad ni stong phrag nyi shu rtsa gcig ro brgya dag dang bcas pa ste/_/re re dag gi tshad ni dus dang mda' yis bsgyur ba'i mi rnams dag gi lo brgya gang yin pa'o/_23_/phra dbugs 
[\[4a.6\]]d+h+ru dang chu tshod nyin zhag d+h+ru dang dus bzhi dag ni stong phrag nyi shu rtsa gcig dang /_/drug brgya rnams ni rim pas lus skyes phra dang mi dang 'byung po lha dang lha min rnams dang ni/_/nus dang gtso bo'i nyin zhag gcig gi tshad de sa gzhi dag la dus bzhi nus pa'i tshad du 'jug_/dum bu dum 
[\[4a.7\]]bur zhi ba'i go 'phang 'khor lo can bgrod nyin zhag gcig la nyi ma bcu gnyis so/_24_/_phyogs dang zla ba mtha' dag dang ni dus dang dus bzhi dang ni me dus bgrod pa rnam pa gnyis/_/rgyal po lo ni mtha' dag rjes su nyin zhag dus sbyor chu tshod dag dang chu yi srang rnams te/_/'di 
[\[4b\]]
[\[4b.1\]]rnams de ltar bgrod pa lus skyes phra dang mi dang 'byung po lha dang lha min rnams dang ni/_/nus dang gtso bo'i zhag re la ste srid pa gsum gyi gnas dang lus kyi dbus su de bzhin no/_25_/lo 'di nas ni drug brgya'i lo yis gsal bar mi bdag grags pa sham+b+ha la zhes bya bar 'byung /_/

[\[4b.3\]]me mkha' rgya mtsho rnams la rab byung la sogs 'das pa rnams bsres kla klo'i lor ni rab tu grub/_/kla klo'i dbang po'i lo ni lag pa gdengs can zla bas dman pa'i lhag ma dag kyang nyi mas bsgyur/_/nag pa la sogs zla ba bsres te 'og tu dus kyis bsgyur ba mkha' me zla bas rnam par dbye/_/
[\[4b.4\]]rnyed pa steng du bsres pa dag ni mi yi bdag po zla ba'i tshogs pa rnam par dag par 'gyur/_27_/zla ba gnas gsum gyur pa'i dbus ma'i phung po dag la gang gnas lag pa rtse mo can gyis bsgyur/_/rtsa ba dag la drug gi chas rnyed de yang bu lon du 'gyur dbus ma la ni sbyang bar bya/_/steng 'og 
[\[4b.5\]]dag las mig dang sum cu'i nor ni bsres pa gsal bar bya ste drug cu'i cha dag gis/_/rnyed pa steng du rab bsres thub pa mchog rnams dag gis bcad pa'i lhag ma dag ni gza' ru 'gyur/_28_/gnas gnyis nyi zla bsres pa dag las dus dang nyi mas thob la 'byung ba bsres pa mtha' dag ni/_/
[\[4b.6\]]de yi steng gi phung por sbyin te dang po lag pas bsgyur la'o brgyad dang gnyis kyis phye ba tshogs/_/gnas gsum dbu ma dag ni dbang phyug gis bsgyur 'og ma dgu dang yon tan rnams kyis cha thob par/_/gnyis bsres dman pa'i dbu mar chu tshod dag gis thob pa nor te steng gi phung por 
[\[4b.7\]]gnyis kyis ni/_29_/bsgyur nas byin la skar mas bgos lhag mi bdag nyi ma'i skar ma la sogs longs spyod dag tu 'gyur/_/gza' la gza' ni rab sbyin mi bdag nyis 'gyur chu tshod rnams te 'og tu tshogs dag la yang gnyis/_/skar mar gnyis dang chu tshod drag po nor du rab gsal

[\[5a.1\]]nyi ma'i longs spyod dag la rab tu sbyin/_/'di dag zla ba'i nges par 'gyur te slar yang zla ba zla ba dag la rab tu sbyin par gsungs/_30_/tshes rnams sbyin dang dor dang slar yang sbyin pa dag ni gza' dang chu tshod rkang pa rnams su ste/_/tshogs kyi cha la chu gter zla 
[\[5a.2\]]bas mnyam dang mi mnyam thob pa dag la sbyin dor rkang pa'i don/_/stong la stong pa rnam dag bcu gsum zla ba'i rkang par lnga ste mig dang nyi ma dag la phyogs/_/bcu gcig me la tshes zhes bya ste bcu dang chu gter rkang par nyi shur gcig gis dman pa'o/_31_/lnga dang bug par nyi

[\[5a.4\]]bas mnyam dang mi mnyam thob la rgyu ba rnams ni sbyin dang dor/_32_/rgyu zhing yod pas lhag ma bsgyur ba las ni dus dang nyi mas cha thob dag kyang de bzhin no/_/de nyid la ni nyi ma'i rab tu dbye bas nyi ma'i rkang pa'i chu tshod sbyin dang dor bar 'gyur/_/gang zhig rig byed rnams kyis tshes 
[\[5a.5\]]bsgyur gsal bar nor te de yi sum cha dag gis bsres pa'o/_/dus kyi rgyu skar rkang pa gsum bcas bu lon dag ste nyi ma'i longs spyod dbus nas sbyang bar bya/_33_/bya ba lhag ma mig dmar ji bzhin ro dus ri bong can rnams dal ba'i las la rkang pa ste/_/longs spyod kyis ni chu tshod

[\[6a\]]
[\[6a.1\]]la sogs pas bgos pa chu tshod do/_40_/mig dmar la ni phyed bcas dgu sbyang gal te bu lon lhag na 'khor lo bsres la rnam par sbyang /_/phyed pas lhag par gyur na'ang 'khor lo phyed ni dor bar bya ste dor ba'i lhag ma rim min 'gyur/_/skar ma drug cus bsgyur te chu 
[\[6a.2\]]tshod dag dang rab tu ldan par gyur pa 'byung me zla bas bgo_/gang thob dal ba'i las dag la yang nor dang bu lon dag ni rim pa min dang rim pas so/_41_/de nyid bco brgyad ri ni rab tu gsal bar ro rkang 'khor lo phyed la steng dang 'og tu ste/_/longs spyod kyis ni chu tshod bsgyur las 
[\[6a.3\]]mda' dang yon tan zla bas 'khor lo phyed las cha thob pa/_/snga ma phyed la gzung byar 'dod de rkang pa'i dbang gis de bzhin phyi ma phyed la dor byar 'gyur/_/zos pa tshogs gcig byas nas gza' yi bgrod pa'i dbang gis sbyin dang dor ba dag kyang chu tshod la'o/_42_/de nas nyi mar sa skyes 
[\[6a.4\]]rnam par sbyang ste bla ma nyi skyes dag kyang myur ba'i las la'o/_/zla skyes dkar po la yang nyi ma dag ste myur ba'i las la nges par rim pas nor du 'gyur/_/sbyangs pa'i lhag mas 'dir ni rkang pa dag ste bza' rgyu rkang pa rnams kyis lhag ma'i chu tshod bsgyur/_/drug cu'i cha yis thob pa bza' rgyu 
[\[6a.5\]]rkang pa dag las gzung bya dang ni dbrog bya de bzhin nyid/_43_/zos pa dag ni tshogs gcig byas nas gza' yi bgrod pa'i dbang gis sbyin dang dor ba mtha' dag ste/_/sa skyes la ni rkang pa rgyal ba sogs te rab gsal ma nu'i gnas la mtha' dag gzhag par bya/_/de ltar gza' rnams 
[\[6a.6\]]thams cad kyi ni rim par yongs su brtsi ba rig par bya'o mi yi dbang /_/zla skyes dkar po las ni nyin mor byed pas rnam dag zla skyes dkar po dag tu yang dag 'gyur/_44_/stong dang nam mkha' zla bas bsgyur ba'i mtha' dag nyin zhag tshogs la me dang nyi ma ri yis dman/_/ri nas phug pa ri dang klu

[\[11a.3\]]ni mi bdag nyis 'gyur chu tshod tshogs la gnyis dang rkang pa mda'/_/'khor lo dag las nges par lha mo drug cu rnams kyis rnam par phye ba dag ni gzar 'gyur ro/_91_/sum cu rtsa gnyis kyis kyang chu tshod thub pa nor gyis bsgyur ba'i lha mo rnams las tshogs la gnyis/_/

[\[11a.6\]]dang myur 'khyog la sogs rgyu ba ste/_/chags bral dang ni thub mchog rigs dang tshangs dang drag po la sogs lha rnams kyis ni shes ma yin/_93_/a sogs lnga yis dbyangs gang dang po'i tshes grangs dbang gis dga' ba la sogs lnga dang ldan gyur pa/_/de dag 'dir ni

[\[16a\]]
[\[16a.1\]]sa la 'obs ni ji srid nyid du 'gro bar 'gyur/_/'obs de bkang nas mkhar gyi rtsig pa dang ni lam srang dag tu 'phral la rab tu 'jug par 'gyur/_138_/stegs bur phur pa gnyis la nges par rgyud dang bcas pa'i gzhu ni g.yo ba med par btags byas nas/_/

[\[17a\]]

[\[20a.1\]]yang mi bdag gnyis kyi so rnams 'byung bar 'gyur/_/slar yang rA ma la ni ltung bar 'gyur te de nyid nag po la ni gsal bar sgyu dag byed/_/sangs rgyas la yang zhi ba dang ni brtul zhugs nges pa byed de rigs ldan la ni 'chi bar 'gyur/_/khu ba

[\[20a.4\]]'gyur/_/phra mo'i rtsa rnams zla ba bzhi pa la ni rkang lag gdong dang mgrin pa dag gi phyogs su ste/_/lnga pa la ni sha dang khrag dang rus pa mkha' ro bsreg za dag ste tshigs rnams dag kyang ngo /_/zla ba drug pa la yang sha dang khrag tu 'gyur te tshor ba bde ba dang ni sdug 
[\[20a.5\]]bsngal yang /_10_/smin ma skra dang spu dang bug pa rnams kyang thub mchog la ste rtsa ni lhag mar gyur pa rnams/_/zla ba'i mjug tu nyin zhag so so'i dbang gis stong pa stong pa mig gi grangs ni skye ba ste/_/gnad dang rus pa rkang du 'gyur te ro 'dzin dang ni gci ba bshang ba brgyad 
[\[20a.6\]]pa dag la yang /_/bug pa zhes la btsa' ba'i dus su skye gnas kyis ni btsir ba dag gis sdug bsngal drag po'o/_11_/mngal du mngal na gnas pa'i sdug bsngal btsa' ba'i dus dang byis pa'i dngos po la yang sdug bsngal te/_/gzhon nu dang ni lang tsho dag la bud med nor dang 
[\[20a.7\]]'byor nyams nyon mongs sdug bsngal chen po gang /_/rgan po nyid la 'chi ba'i sdug bsngal slar yang 'gro ba drug tu 'jigs ster ngu 'bod la sogs te/_/sgyu mas rmongs par byas pa'i mtha' dag 'gro ba 'di ni sdug bsngal dag las sdug bsngal 'dzin par byed/_12_/'khor bar mi 
[\[20b\]]
[\[20b.1\]]nyid du ni 'gyur ba dag kyang 'ga' zhig tu ste chos kyi blo ni res 'ga' na/_/de bas sangs rgyas la ni rjes chags dge ba'i dbang gis 'gyur zhing dang po'i theg par rab tu 'jug_/de bas dpal ldan rdo rje theg pa sgom pa la ni blo gros ma lus brgya lam dag ni 'jug_/de las 
[\[20b.2\]]sangs rgyas nyid 'dod mchog gi bde ba'i go 'phang dag la 'jug pa e ma shin tu dka'/_13_/mngal du yang dag dag pa'i sku ste btsa' ba'i dus nas so ni skyes pa dag kyang chos kyi'o/_/so rnams rab tu lhung bar 'gyur ba mi yi bdag po rgyal ba yi ni longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku/_/
[\[20b.3\]]de nas sprul pa'i sku ni rab tu gsal zhing nges par 'chi ba'i mtshams te mi rnams dag gi'o/_/mngal dang phyi rol dag tu slar yang 'di ni ye shes rnam shes dbye bas rnam pa bzhi ru 'gyur/_14_/rnam shes zla ba nyi ma dag kyang pad+mar gnas pa de ni mngal du dag pa'i sku yin te/_/
[\[20b.4\]]khams dang phung po rab tu 'byung ba rnam pa bcu po gang yin rgyal ba yi ni chos kyi sku/_/de nas yon tan dbang gis rna ba sgra sogs skyes pa dag ni longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku ru 'gyur/_/de nas skra la sogs pa skyes te rab tu btsa' ba'i dus kyang 'dir ni sprul pa dag gi sku/_15_/
[\[20b.5\]]skyes pa na ni nges par dbugs dag gang zhig rab tu 'byung bar 'gyur ba de nyid dag pa'i sku/_/de nas so ni rab 'byung gang zhig tshig ni gsal min dag kyang de bzhin nyid du chos kyi sku/_/de nas gnyis skyes rnams ni ltung bar 'gyur ba mi yi bdag po rgyal ba'i longs spyod 
[\[20b.6\]]rdzogs pa'i sku/_/so rnams dag las 'chi ba'i mtshams ni phyi ru rgyal ba'i bdag po dag gi sprul pa'i sku ru 'gyur/_16_/mngal du 'dir ni dang po dpal ldan sku yi rdo rjer 'gyur te gsung gi rang bzhin skyes pa na'o/_/thugs ni so dag rab tu skyes pa nyid na'o slar yang lhung 
[\[20b.7\]]nas ye shes rdo rjer rab tu 'gyur/_/ye shes rnam shes bsres shing nyi ma ri bong can dang bcas pa ye shes rdo rje la sogs kun/_/mngal na gnas pa dag ni sku yi rdo rje la sogs rim pa yis kyang stobs dang ldan par 'gyur/_17_/sa yi snying po dag ni bsreg zar 'gyur te 
[\[21a\]]
[\[21a.1\]]'dzin ma dang ni rlung dag stong pa'i snying po'o/_/rlung dang sa yi snying po dag ni chu nyid du 'gyur rang bzhin med pa dang ni rang bzhin no/_/sa yi rten ni chu ste 'bar byed dag kyang chu yi rlung ni dag par byed pa dag gi'o/_/rlung gi stong par rab tu 'gyur te 
[\[21a.2\]]mi yi dbang po 'byung ba'i rten dang brten pa ji lta bzhin/_18_/phung po'i rten ni 'byung ba rnams su 'gyur te lus mchog dag la 'byung ba'i tshogs kyi rtsa rnams so/_/rtsa rnams kyi ni srog rlung rnam pa bcu por 'gyur te srog gi rlung rnams dag gi sems yin no/_/sems de 
[\[21a.3\]]gnyis kyi rang bzhin du 'gyur yon tan dbang gis rang bzhin can dang rang bzhin med pa'o/_/de dag gi yang rten ni gtso bo srid pa gsum gyis btud cing gzhom med kun tu 'gro pa gang /_19_/dri ni 'dzin ma dag las 'byung ste ro yang rtse mo can las gzugs kyi khams ni 
[\[21a.4\]]chu las so/_/rlung las reg par 'gyur te zhi ba las kyang sgra dang chos kyi khams ni nam mkha' dag las so/_/stong pa sgra ni 'dzin par byed de nges par rgyal ba skyed mdzad chos kyi khams dang rlung dag ni/_/dri dang me yang gzugs te chu dag kyang ni ro'o de bzhin 'dzin ma dag 
[\[21a.5\]]ni reg bya'o/_20_/grub pa gzugs ni 'dzin ma la ste srid pa gsum gyi gnas su chu la yang ni 'du shes grub/_/me la nges pa nyid du tshor bar 'gyur te rlung la yang ni phung po 'du byed kho na'o/_/rnam shes ye shes bsres pa nam mkha' dang bcas rdo rje dag la grub pa nyid 
[\[21a.6\]]de lus 'di la/_/khams dang phung po la sogs thams cad 'gyur ba dag kyang rdul dang byang chub sems sogs sbyor bas so/_21_/rna ba rdo rje'i rang bzhin du 'gyur mi yi bdag po sems ni nam mkha' dag gi rang bzhin no/_/sna ni sa yi rang bzhin dag ste de bzhin kho na lce ni me yi 
[\[21a.7\]]rang bzhin nyid du 'gyur/_/mig ni chu yi rang bzhin dag ste srid pa gsum gyi gnas su lus kyang rlung gi rang bzhin no/_/de ltar rna ba la sogs thams cad yon tan dbang gis 'gyur te ye shes rnam shes sbyor bas so/_22_/ser sna ye shes khams la 'gyur te lus mchog 
[\[21b\]]
[\[21b.1\]]dag la nam mkha' la ni zhe sdang rdo rje'o/_/rlung la phrag dog rdo rje me la chags pa'i rdo rje rab tu gsal mdzad de bzhin nyid du ni/_/chu la dpal ldan nga rgyal rdo rje sa la gti mug rdo rje rang bzhin yon tan dbang gis so/_/ngag dang lag pa rkang pa bshang lam thams cad rlung me 
[\[21b.2\]]chu dang sa rnams dag las 'byung bar 'gyur/_23_/thur sel ye shes khams la rnam gsum zhes par 'gyur te srog gi rlung yang stong pa dag la'o/_/rlung gi dbus su mnyam gnas rtse mo can la slar yang gyen rgyu chu la khyab par byed pa nyid/_/sa la klu ste de nas rus sbal 
[\[21b.3\]]rtsangs pa dang ni lha sbyin nor las rgyal ba dag gi rlung /_/bzhi po rlung dang me dag dang ni chu dang sa rnams dag la rim pa yis ni yang dag 'gyur/_24_/gtsug tor stong pa'i khams la 'gyur te lha dang mi rnams kyi ni ye shes khams la gsang ba'o/_/snying gi pad+ma rlung 
[\[21b.4\]]gi khams la rab gsal rtse mo can la mgrin pa'i 'khor lo gsal bar ro/_/chu la smin ma'i nang gi pad+ma nor ni nor gyi 'dab ldan lte ba'i pad+ma sa la'o/_/rkang pa lag pa dag gi tshigs drug sa dang chu dang bsreg za rlung rnams dag la gsum gyi grangs/_25_/sa sogs 
[\[21b.5\]]khams lnga dag la rnam gsum yon tan dbang gis 'byung ba rkang pa lag pa dag gi ni/_/mthe bong la sogs sor mo bcu ni gsum gyis bsgyur ba'i tshigs kyi gzugs dang ldan pa mtha' dag ste/_/de dag sen mo'i mthar thug yang dag gzung bya slar yang so rnams de 
[\[21b.6\]]nyid rab tu dbye bas so/_/gzhan yang spu yi mthar ni lus mchog la 'gyur de dang de ni de bzhin du yang khams la'o/_26_/gsang ba gtsug tor lte ba la yang lhan cig skyes pa rgyal ba'i sku ste rang bzhin med rang bzhin/_/snying ga'i chu skyes la ni chos skur 'gyur te 
[\[21b.7\]]mgrin pa'i chu skyes la yang longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku/_/thig le la yang sprul pa'i sku ni yon tan dbang gis lhag pa'i lha ru rim pa dag gis 'gyur/_/snying ga'i 'khor lo mgrin pa'i 'khor lo spyi bo'i pad+ma chos dang longs spyod rdzogs dang dag pa'o/_27_/lte ba mgrin pa 
[\[22a\]]
[\[22a.1\]]dag dang gsang ba spyi bo dang ni snying ga de bzhin gtsug tor dbus su ni/_/yum dang gtso bo'i sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi rdo rje rnam pa gsum po dag kyang rim pas 'gyur/_/'khor lo rin chen mkha' yi pad+ma chu skyes ral gri mchog dang rdo rje dang ldan rigs 
[\[22a.2\]]drug go/_/de dag steng 'og rtsa gsum rnam gsum lam la gnas pa rnams te nyi zla me yi dbye bas so/_28_/lus la bsam pa rab tu zhugs pa nam mkha' bzhin du ro mnyam bsam pa'i dbus su lus dag kyang /_/rnal 'byor ldan pas shes bya rang bzhin yon tan dbang 
[\[22a.3\]]gis khams kyi kha dog la sogs dbye ba ste/_/ser po nag po dag dang kha dog dmar po zhes dang dkar po sa rlung me dang chu la'o/_/ye shes nam mkha' la yang sngon po ljang gur 'gyur te lus dang bsam pa'i rab tu dbye bas so/_29_/shes rab thabs ni rus pa sha dang chu dang 
[\[22a.4\]]bcas pa'i khrag dang me dag dang ni gci ba nyid/_/rlung dang bshang ba stong pa khu ba mchog gi lus la dpal ldan rdul dang sems nyid dag tu 'gyur/_/rna ba sgra sogs gzhan rnams rang gi rgyal ba'i rigs kyi dbang gis ngag gi rang bzhin la sogs kyang /_/de ltar lha 
[\[22a.5\]]mo sangs rgyas dang bcas yul dang yul can rnams ni dkyil 'khor dag la rig par bya/_30_/rtsa rnams stong phrag drug ni nyin mor byed pas bsgyur ba mngal gyi dbus dag tu yang 'phel ba ni/_/byas nas lo ni rdzogs pa na yang gtong ste ri bong 'dzin pa nyi ma dag kyang 
[\[22a.6\]]'phel bar byed/_/lo ni bcu gnyis dag na nyin byed khams ni 'phel ba gtong ste rang gi lus po dag la'o/_/rtsa ni 'chad pa rab tu skyes nas nyin zhag nyin zhag dag la rim pas zung gi grangs su 'gyur/_31_/dpal ldan 'byung ba dang ni dbang po rnams dag bcu 
[\[22a.7\]]ru rab 'gyur las kyi dbang po lnga rnams dang /_/de tsam lnga ru brjod pa rnams dang yon tan bcas pa dag kyang yid dang blo dang ngar 'dzin mchog_/'di dag de nyid rnams te lus dang srid pa gsum gyi gnas su 'di ni longs spyod gtso bo dag_/phung po lnga yi bdag nyid 
[\[22b\]]
[\[22b.1\]]rnam gsum srid pa la gnas bdag gi las ni bsags pa dag gi'o/_32_/sa ni sra ba chu ni gsher ba me yang tsha ba rlung ni yang ba nyid de bug pa ni/_/stong pa dag ste brtan pa'i rus pa 'chi med ri bo mchog dang char pa 'dir ni bdud rtsir 'gyur/_/rdo rje sgra dang gzhu yang bshang 
[\[22b.2\]]ba dag ste chu tshod rnams ni dbugs dang dbugs 'byung zung dag go/_/zung rnams dgu brgya nyis 'gyur dag gis 'pho ba gcig ste skar rtsis pa rnams bug pa'i dbye ba'o/_33_/rgyu skar dag ni so yi phreng ba rjes su nyin zhag chu tshod rnams ni tshigs kyi dbye bar 'gyur/_/rtsa rnams 
[\[22b.3\]]chu bo rgyu ma sprin te ri bong can gyi cha gzhan khams rnams gnyis kyi rang bzhin no/_/spu dang shig dang khu ba srin bu'i rigs dang bcas pa rnams ni 'byung po skye gnas rnam pa bzhi/_/dga' ba la sogs mthe bong tshigs rnams spyi bo'i pad+ma lte ba'i pad+ma dag kyang nus pa'o/_34_/
[\[22b.4\]]tshangs pa'i yul sa mtho ris 'jig rten lag mchog rkang pa dag ni mi yul sa 'og 'jig rten no/_/chu gter gling dang ri bo rnams ni gsher dang 'jam pa sra ba gsum gyi rang bzhin khams rnams so/_/lan tshwa'i chu gter gci ba 'di dag rnams kyi me rlung dkyil 'khor rgyal po 
[\[22b.5\]]khrag dang pags pa'o/_/skra rnams grub pa mtha' dag srid pa gsum gyi gnas su du ma'i dbye ba dag gis grub pa'o/_35_/snying mgrin lte ba'i pad+ma dag la rdo rje nyi ma rtse mo can rnams rab 'phro rim pa yis/_/gzhu yi rnam pa zlum po dang ni nyin bzhin sreg byed chu gter zur gyi 
[\[22b.6\]]thab khung dag la yang /_/de dag gang na me gzhan dri med 'od zer 'phro ba ye shes gzugs ni mun pa dag gi mthar/_/gang zhig nyi ma glog gis mi gdung ri bong 'dzin pa gza' dang skar ma la sogs med pa na'o/_36_/gza' ni snying ga'i pad+ma la ste rab tu gsal byas chu 
[\[22b.7\]]tshod drug cu lte ba'i pad+ma la/_/smin ma'i dbus su nyin zhag rkang pa ma nu slar yang mi bdag longs spyod 'khor lor skar ma'o/_/slar yang lte ba'i pad+mar rgyu skar rnams kyi chu tshod rnal 'byor ldan pa dag gis rig par bya/_/chu tshod chu srang gsal bar mtha' dag lus la lag pa 
[\[23a\]]
[\[23a.1\]]rkang pa la sogs dag gi tshigs la'o/_37_/lte ba'i chu skyes nyi ma'i 'dab ma dag la 'khor te mchog gi cha ni 'pho ba rim pas so/_/srog zung dgu brgya nyis 'gyur dag gis 'pho bar 'gyur te dus sbyor karka Ta nas mi yi dbang /_/dus sbyor gang la nyi ma gnas 
[\[23a.2\]]pas nyin dang mtshan mor 'khor ba de la de ni rig par bya/_/dus sbyor tshad dang nor dang bu lon mnyam pa dag kyang bgrod pa g.yas dang g.yon la shes par bya/_38_/re re pad+ma'i 'dab ma dag la rab tu 'bab ste nub tu 'dzin ma dang ni byang du chu/_/lho ru me dang shar du rlung ni rab 
[\[23a.3\]]tu 'bab ste 'dab ma'i dbus dag tu yang stong pa'o/_/dbye ba lnga yis 'pho bar 'gyur te mnyam pa rab tu 'bab pa dman pa'i dbugs ni drug cu'o/_/de ltar 'pho ba'i dbye ba rnams ni nyin zhag so so'i dus su dus sbyor dbye ba dag gis 'gyur/_39_/stong pa la sogs de nyid

[\[23a.6\]]rtsa ni ri bong mtshan ma 'bab ste nges par dkar po g.yas su dmar po nyi ma'o/_/dbus su dus me'i gzugs ni rab tu 'bab ste mnyam pa dag la dman pa'i dbugs ni drug cu'o/_41_/srog dang thur sel mnyam gnas pad+ma nor gyi 'dab ma dag la rlung ni gyen du rgyu ba dang /_/khyab 
[\[23a.7\]]byed klu dang rus sbal dag dang rtsangs pa'i rlung dang lha sbyin dang ni nor las rgyal zhes pa/_/rlung ni rnam pa bcu po 'di rnams lte ba'i 'khor lo la ni las kyi dbye bas yang dag gnas/_/dung can mtha' yang i Da la sogs rang gi snying ga'i pad+ma rtsa yi 'khor lo mtha' dag go/_42_/
[\[23b\]]
[\[23b.1\]]srog ni nyi ma zla ba'i lam na gnas pa dag kyang srog byed bza' dang btung ba ma lus pa/_/de dag thur sel gyis ni 'og tu 'dren te mtha' dag ro mnyam lus la mnyam gnas dag gis 'thob/_/lus la gyen rgyus g.yo bar byed de kha dang lag pa rkang pa rnams kyis glu

[\[23b.3\]]gis te glal ba dag kyang lha sbyin gyis/_/lus ni lnga pa nyid gyur gtong bar mi byed mi yi bdag po rlung gcig nor las rgyal ba'o/_/de ltar srog la sogs pa'i rlung rnams thams cad rang bzhin gyis gnas yon tan dag ni gtong mi byed/_44_/dmar ser phra mo dag kyang 
[\[23b.4\]]i Da la sogs rnam gsum lam bgrod snar ma dang ni lag ldan lce/_/drug pa rgyal dang ri yang rgyal ba nor dang dgu pa a laM bu Sha dpal ldan ku hA'o/_/phyogs kyi grangs ni dung can ma gang mi yi bdag po byang chub sa bon bde ba'i mthar zag byed/_/dpal ldan rtsa yi 'khor 
[\[23b.5\]]lo 'di ni rnam pa mang por 'gyur te tshigs kyi dbye ba du mas so/_45_/dbang po me dang gshin rje lha min chur bcas rlung dang gnod sbyin dang ni drag po dag gi dbus/_/gnas ni 'di dag rnams su de yi rang bzhin brten zhing nus pa nyin dang mtshan mor 'khor bar byed/_/de yi steng gi

[\[24a\]]
[\[24a.1\]]po'i mthar thug mtha' dag go/_47_/'khor lo can ni rang gi lus la rdo rje can te lha mchog bdag rnams yan lag bcu gnyis 'gog_/rigs ldan dag ni yang dag ye shes glang po rta dang shing rta mngag gzhug 'phags pa'i tshad med rnams/_/drag po rang 
[\[24a.2\]]rgyal ming can dag tu 'gyur te ha nu mAn ni nyan thos gdug pa kla klo'i dbang /_/srog chags rnams kyi sdig pa dag ste gang zhig byed pa'i blo gros sdug bsngal sbyin byed mi dge'i lam/_48_/rta la gnas pa ma rig pa ste lha min dpung rnams mtha' dag bdud kyi phyogs ni rnam pa bzhi/_/

[\[24a.5\]]'khrug par 'gyur te nges par lus can rnams kyi lus kyi dbus su'o/_/sgyu ma'i gzugs kyang phyi rol tu ni nges par mkha'i yul du kla klo 'khrug ste 'khrug pa min/_50_/dus kyi lo ni me grangs lo yi nyin zhag tshogs kyis bsgyur te rigs drug bgrod pa'i yan lag gis/_/zla

[\[24b\]]
[\[24b.1\]]de dag phyed kyi dbye bas rnam par phye ba ste/_/slar yang bsres pa dag ni dbye ba gnyis te bcu gsum bcu dgu dang ni bug pa de nyid bcas/_52_/zla ba'i cha shas su nges nyin zhag tshogs las gza' yi cha yis thob pa gang dag nyin zhag drug_/de dag rdo rje can gyi phyag

[\[24b.3\]]gzugs sogs mig la sogs pa drug ste slar yang mi yi bdag po lha yi tshogs ni drug gnyis so/_/rnal 'byor ma dang klu dang gtum mo brgyad ces bya ste srid pa 'di dag thams cad nyis 'gyur ro/_/'di dag dpal ldan dus kyi 'khor lo bzhi lhag brgya ste de yi phyed

[\[61a.3\]]lha min la sogs rnams kyi de bzhin nor bdag rtse mo can dang chu gter rlung dang tshogs kyi ni/_/glang po'i dbang po dang ni lug dang chu srin zhes dang [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]dang ni byi ba rim pas so/_40_/b+he ruN+Da dang khrung khrung mig sngon bshang lam gdong ni bya rog gdong la sogs pa'i zur du ste/_/

[\[79a.1\]]dag tu brtul zhugs nges pa brgya dang bsnyung gnas ri las mchong ba dang /_/g.yul dang nyi ma gza' yis zin la yul gyi bde 'dod du ma'i mtshon dang me la mchong bas so/_218_/bdud rnams dag gis khrag ni btung ba'i slad du 'di dag mtha' dag sngon gyi tshe yang

[\[106a.3\]]mchod 'os so/_249_/sdig can gang yin gang zhig dpal ldan bla ma'i zhabs pad bzang po dag la dus gsum phyag mi byed/_/mi shes gang yin gang zhig rnam mang me tog rnams kyis dus gsum du ni maN+Dal mi byed pa/_/myur du mnar med 'gro ba gang yin gang zhig bde mnyam ster byed bla ma skyo 
[\[106a.4\]]ba skyed pa po/_/shes rab ye shes thob pa gang yin ma nyams gang zhig mchog gi bla ma'i zhabs ni mi spong ba/_250_/nyams pa gang yin gang zhig rtsa gsum dag la gnas pa'i rlung ni mtha' yas dus su bsad pa'o/_/dpa' bo gang yin gang zhig mnyam dang mi mnyam lam la srog dang thur sel 
[\[106a.5\]]rlung ni gsod byed pa'o/_/sbyin bdag gang yin gang zhig dpal ldan bla mar rang lus bu dang chung ma la sogs thams cad ster/_/ma rabs gang yin gang zhig slu byed rang gi snying gi sdig pas drangs pa'i sems dang g.yo sgyu can/_251_/dpal ldan dpal gyi chos kyi 'khor lo lha yi mchog 
[\[106a.6\]]gis btud pa'i gdan ni kha dog sna tshogs pa/_/de la sangs rgyas lha dang mi yi bla ma nye bar bzhugs pas zla ba'i zhu ba rtogs pa na/_/sems can don gyi slad du mi yi bdag po rgyud kyi rgyal po dang po'i sangs rgyas gsungs pa gang /_/de yang da lta bdag gis de rnams A li kA li nyung ngu'i rgyud 
[\[106a.7\]]du bkod do nyi ma kye/_252_/sangs rgyas kyis ni gsungs pa dag las dus kyi 'khor lo bdag gis phreng 'dzin sdeb sbyor bcings pas brjod/_/pad+ma dkar po rdo rje 'dzin pa khyod kyis 'di ni sngags pa rnams la bstan pa nyid du mdzod/_/ji ltar nyi ma dang bcas thub pa'i rigs ni shes rab ye shes 
[\[106b\]]
[\[106b.1\]]thob pa 'di las byung gyur pa/_/rnam gsum srid pa la gnas sems can rnams ni dus kyi 'khor lo'i drin las de bzhin 'gyur bar shog_253_/nyi ma khyod dam mi dbang phyi mar gyur pa'i srid par dus kyi 'khor lo'i rnal 'byor gcig las ni/_/tshangs pa'i rigs 'di las skyes mi yi bdag po grags pa bdag ni 
[\[106b.2\]]'jam dbyangs bdag gi dga' ma ni/_/dpal ldan sgrol ma pad dkar can ni yon tan kun ster 'jig rten mgon po chu skyes mtshan ma can/_/ut+pal sdong bu yis ni rang gzhan la gnas srid pa thams cad don ni ji lta bzhin du ltos/_254_/ut+pal sdong bu yis ni rang gzhan la gnas srid pa thams cad don ni 
[\[106b.3\]]ji lta bzhin mthong nas/_/thub pa'i dbang po rnams dang lhan cig nyi mas cod pan bcas pa'i mgo dang lag pa dag gis ni/_/rigs ldan zhabs kyi pad+mar phyag dag 'tshal nas smras pa ston pa gcig pu mchog gi thugs rje yis/_/thub pa'i rigs ni 'jigs rung 'khor ba'i sdug bsngal dag las phyung nas ye shes 
[\[106b.4\]]lam la gzhag par mdzod/_255_/khyod ni rgan yang gzhon nu mtha' dag rgyal ba'i sras po yin yang thog mar dang po'i sangs rgyas khyod/_/bud med sbyor zhing tshangs par spyod ldan mchog gi thugs rje dag gis 'jig rten gnyen te gshin rje'i dgra/_/khyod ni zhi yang rdo rje bzang po 'chi ba'i 'jigs pa 'phrog pa khyod 
[\[106b.5\]]ni rtag tu bdud kyi bdud/_/khyod ni grol yang srid pa 'dir ni 'gro ba smin pa'i don du grags pa khyod ni rab tu 'jug_256_/tshangs pa'i rigs las nyi ma bdag ni thub pa'i rigs kyis btud par gyur pa zhabs kyi rtsa bar lhung /_/sems can rnams kyi thar pa'i slad du dus kyi 'khor lo mtha' dag sa la rab tu gsal 
[\[106b.6\]]bar byas/_/rigs ldan rigs la khyod ni nyi ma sa bdag gis btud dpal ldan grags pa dpal ldan ka lA par/_/khyod kyi zhabs kyi pad+ma dag la 'jigs rung 'khor bas 'jigs pa dag ni skyabs su lhag par mchi/_257_/sa ga'i zla ba'i nya la mtshan mo'i dus ni 'das shing nyin par rab tu ma zhugs pa dag la/_/
[\[106b.7\]]thub pa'i skye bo dang bcas nyi ma sna tshogs gtso bos byin gyis brlabs pas phyag rgya'i dngos grub thob/_/sems can rnams kyi thar pa'i slad du bdag gi thugs kyi rdo rje mtha' dag sa la gnas pa bzhin/_/dus kyi 'khor lo'i mthu las sems can nyid rnams dag gi rnam gsum srid pa dag la gnas gyur cig_258_/
[\[107a\]]
[\[107a.1\]]sems can kun don mdzad pa po yis mchog gi thugs rje dag gis rgyud kyi don ni bstan mdzad nas/_/sras po pad+ma dkar po rab mchog yon tan dag gi gter la gnang ba stsal ba nges par ni/_/dang por khyod kyis rgyud kyi rgyal po mtha' dag ma tshang med pa glegs bam dag la gnas pa 
[\[107a.2\]]dang /_/de nas rang gi 'dod pas 'grel bshad byas nas gzhan gyi gnas su bgrod par bya'o 'jig rten gnyen/_259_/gang dag steng na bdud kyi phyogs gnas lha ma yin rnams mchog tu 'jigs byed byang chub sems dpa' rnams/_/mi yi 'jig rten dag na phyogs dang phyogs bral du gnas khro bo'i rgyal 
[\[107a.3\]]po btsun mor bcas pa rnams/_/gang dag sa 'og dag na gdengs can dbang po gdon tshogs mi dge thams cad dus su 'ching byed pa/_/de dag thams cad nyin zhag so so'i dus su mi shes 'jig rten kun nas skyong bar byed gyur cig_260_/rgyud ni dpal ldan dus kyi 'khor lo shin tu bsdus pa ma lus rdo rje 
[\[107a.4\]]sems dpa' lhag pa'i lha/_/thabs dang shes rab gcig sbyor rgyal ba'i bdag po'i rdo rje dag gis de nyid rnam pa bcu drug pa/_/sems can rnams kyi thar pa'i slad du rgyal ba'i bdag pos gsungs shing bdag gis kyang ni bstan pa gang /_/bsod nams de yis sems can thams cad 'gyur med mchog 
[\[107a.5\]]gi bde bar gyur cig de la 'dud gyur cig_261_/dang po'i sangs rgyas stong phrag bcu gnyis pa las phyung ba dpal dus kyi 'khor lo las ye shes kyi le'u ste lnga pa'o//_//mchog gi dang po'i sangs rgyas las phyung ba/_rgyud kyi rgyal po dpal dus kyi 'khor lo zhes bya ba rdzogs so//_//kha che'i paN+Di ta so ma nA tha 
[\[107a.6\]]dang /_bod kyi lo tsA ba 'bro dge slong shes rab grags kyis bsgyur cing zhus te gtan la phab pa las/_dus phyis yon tan phul du byung ba dpag tu med pas spras pa'i bla ma dam pa chos kyi rgyal po'i bka' lung dang /_dpon chen shAkya bzang po'i gsung bzhin du/_mkhas pa chen po zhang ston mdo sde dpal dang /_dus kyi 'khor lo'i tshul
